# Liraasa 2.3 grammar specification: the parse test, every example

Companion to `review/grammar_spec_2_3.md` §6. Generated from `handoff/liraasa_2_3_llm.json`: 1471 example strings.

Words are segmented by the editor's reader (`editor/checker/reader.js`, in the order of `how_to_translate.how_a_reader_parses_one_word`) and placed in the 20-slot template by `editor/checker/slots.js`, whose rules also run. The specification's own judgements are added by hand on top: lines the JSON marks as album readings, the ambiguities in the issue log, and contradictions with a stated rule. The reader always prefers a lexical reading, so the homophones it hides are added back from the issue log.

**Verdicts.** PASS: one parse. PASS (lexical homophone): one structure, but a word has two readings with nearly the same meaning (I-16, I-17). MULTIPLE PARSES: two structures, or a licence that depends on an undefined rule. FAIL: an unknown word, a checker error, or a line that breaks a stated rule.

**Slot labels.** SUBJ, OBJ, COMP (copula complement), ADJN (adjunct), V, ACTOR (after a valency operator), FRAME, TOPIC, VOC (vocative), LINK (clause linker), Q (final ki), FINAL (attitude, status or reciprocal word), operator slots by name (TENSE, MODAL, ASPECT …), NEG. · = no slot (inside a fragment, or a modifier the placer leaves inside its phrase). The checker's placer is an implementation of the template, not the grammar; where its labels disagree with the axioms, the axioms are the reference. Morphs: word[kind:role|class<root+suffixes]; roles n noun, v verb, a adjective, an ascended noun, aa ascended adjective, nn negative noun, na negative adjective.

| Verdict | Count |
| --- | --- |
| PASS (lexical homophone) | 476 |
| PASS | 937 |
| FAIL | 26 |
| MULTIPLE PARSES | 32 |
